Our approach leverages that RGB-depth images provide samples of a surface light field. By encoding random subsamples of this surface light field into a compact set of latent vectors, our model learns to represent both geometry and appearance within a unified 3D latent space. This representation reproduces view-dependent effects such as specular highlights and Fresnel reflections under complex lighting. We further train a latent flow matching model on this representation to learn its distribution conditioned on a single input image, enabling the generation of 3D objects with appearances consistent with the lighting and materials in the input. Experiments show that our approach achieves higher visual quality and better input fidelity than existing methods.

</details>


### [75] [COMIC: Agentic Sketch Comedy Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11048)
*Susung Hong,Brian Curless,Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman,Steve Seitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个全自动AI系统，生成类似《周六夜现场》的短喜剧视频，通过基于真实制作工作室角色的智能体群体进行迭代竞争、评估和改进，利用LLM评论家分析YouTube喜剧视频来自动评估幽默效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI视频生成系统在创意内容创作，特别是需要复杂叙事结构和幽默元素的喜剧视频方面存在局限。传统方法难以自动评估幽默质量，需要开发能够理解观众偏好并生成高质量喜剧内容的自动化系统。

Method: 系统采用基于真实制作工作室角色的智能体群体架构，包括创意生成、评估和改进等角色。关键创新是引入LLM评论家，通过分析YouTube喜剧视频语料库来对齐真实观众偏好，自动评估幽默效果。系统通过迭代竞争、评估和改进过程优化创意质量和多样性。

Result: 实验表明，该框架生成的视频质量接近专业制作的喜剧小品，在视频生成方面展示了最先进的性能。系统能够自动评估幽默效果，并产生多样化的高质量喜剧内容。

Conclusion: 提出的全自动AI系统能够生成高质量喜剧视频，通过基于真实制作流程的智能体架构和LLM驱动的幽默评估机制，实现了接近专业水准的喜剧内容创作，为创意视频生成提供了新方法。

Abstract: We propose a fully automated AI system that produces short comedic videos similar to sketch shows such as Saturday Night Live. Starting with character references, the system employs a population of agents loosely based on real production studio roles, structured to optimize the quality and diversity of ideas and outputs through iterative competition, evaluation, and improvement. A key contribution is the introduction of LLM critics aligned with real viewer preferences through the analysis of a corpus of comedy videos on YouTube to automatically evaluate humor. Our experiments show that our framework produces results approaching the quality of professionally produced sketches while demonstrating state-of-the-art performance in video generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Targeted Bit-Flip Attacks on LLM-Based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10042)
*Jialai Wang,Ya Wen,Zhongmou Liu,Yuxiao Wu,Bingyi He,Zongpeng Li,Ee-Chien Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Flip-Agent是首个针对LLM智能体的目标比特翻转攻击框架，能够操纵最终输出和工具调用，在真实世界智能体任务中显著优于现有攻击方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标比特翻转攻击主要针对单步推理模型（如图像分类器），而具有多阶段流水线和外部工具的LLM智能体系统存在新的攻击面，这一领域尚未被探索

Method: 提出Flip-Agent框架，专门针对LLM智能体设计的目标比特翻转攻击方法，能够同时操纵智能体的最终输出和工具调用行为

Result: 实验表明Flip-Agent在真实世界智能体任务中显著优于现有目标比特翻转攻击方法，揭示了LLM智能体系统的关键安全漏洞

Conclusion: LLM智能体系统存在新的安全威胁面，Flip-Agent框架成功展示了针对这类系统的有效攻击方法，强调了加强LLM智能体安全防护的重要性

Abstract: Targeted bit-flip attacks (BFAs) exploit hardware faults to manipulate model parameters, posing a significant security threat. While prior work targets single-step inference models (e.g., image classifiers), LLM-based agents with multi-stage pipelines and external tools present new attack surfaces, which remain unexplored. This work introduces Flip-Agent, the first targeted BFA framework for LLM-based agents, manipulating both final outputs and tool invocations. Our experiments show that Flip-Agent significantly outperforms existing targeted BFAs on real-world agent tasks, revealing a critical vulnerability in LLM-based agent systems.

</details>


### [77] [SBOMs into Agentic AIBOMs: Schema Extensions, Agentic Orchestration, and Reproducibility Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10057)
*Petar Radanliev,Carsten Maple,Omar Santos,Kayvan Atefi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于多智能体架构的主动式AI物料清单（AIBOM）框架，将传统的静态SBOM扩展为能够捕获运行时行为、环境漂移和可利用性上下文的动态溯源工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链安全需要支持动态执行条件下可重现性和漏洞评估的溯源机制。传统的软件物料清单（SBOM）只能提供静态依赖清单，无法捕获运行时行为、环境漂移或可利用性上下文。

Method: 提出了一个基于多智能体架构的AIBOM框架，包含三个核心智能体：1）基线环境重建智能体（MCP）；2）运行时依赖和漂移监控智能体（A2A）；3）策略感知漏洞和VEX推理智能体（AGNTCY）。这些智能体通过结合运行时执行证据、依赖使用和环境缓解措施，生成上下文可利用性断言，并使用ISO/IEC 20153:2025 CSAF v2.0语义表达。框架对CycloneDX和SPDX进行了最小化、标准对齐的模式扩展。

Result: 在异构分析工作负载上的评估表明，与现有溯源系统相比，该框架在运行时依赖捕获、可重现性保真度和漏洞解释稳定性方面均有改进，且计算开销较低。消融研究证实每个智能体都提供了确定性自动化无法实现的独特能力。

Conclusion: AIBOM框架通过自主、策略约束的推理将SBOM扩展为主动溯源工件，能够更好地支持软件供应链安全，特别是在动态执行条件下的可重现性和漏洞评估方面。

Abstract: Software supply-chain security requires provenance mechanisms that support reproducibility and vulnerability assessment under dynamic execution conditions. Conventional Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) provide static dependency inventories but cannot capture runtime behaviour, environment drift, or exploitability context. This paper introduces agentic Artificial Intelligence Bills of Materials (AIBOMs), extending SBOMs into active provenance artefacts through autonomous, policy-constrained reasoning. We present an agentic AIBOM framework based on a multi-agent architecture comprising (i) a baseline environment reconstruction agent (MCP), (ii) a runtime dependency and drift-monitoring agent (A2A), and (iii) a policy-aware vulnerability and VEX reasoning agent (AGNTCY). These agents generate contextual exploitability assertions by combining runtime execution evidence, dependency usage, and environmental mitigations with ISO/IEC 20153:2025 Common Security Advisory Framework (CSAF) v2.0 semantics. Exploitability is expressed via structured VEX assertions rather than enforcement actions. The framework introduces minimal, standards-aligned schema extensions to CycloneDX and SPDX, capturing execution context, dependency evolution, and agent decision provenance while preserving interoperability. Evaluation across heterogeneous analytical workloads demonstrates improved runtime dependency capture, reproducibility fidelity, and stability of vulnerability interpretation compared with established provenance systems, with low computational overhead. Ablation studies confirm that each agent contributes distinct capabilities unavailable through deterministic automation.

</details>


### [78] [OAuthHub: Mitigating OAuth Data Overaccess through a Local Data Hub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10056)
*Qiyu Li,Yuhe Tian,Haojian Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OAuthHub是一个开发框架，利用用户个人设备作为OAuth数据共享的中介控制器，通过声明式数据访问和三种明确访问模式来减少不必要的数据暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OAuth服务提供商（如Google、Microsoft）仅提供粗粒度的数据访问权限，导致第三方应用即使开发者希望最小化数据访问，也不得不获取超出必要范围的用户数据。

Method: OAuthHub框架包含三个关键创新：1）识别出大多数OAuth应用只需要在三个明确定义的时刻访问数据（安装时、用户操作响应时、预定间隔时）；2）要求开发者明确声明预期数据访问，并通过间歇可用的个人设备支持三种常见访问模式；3）提供跨提供商的集中运行时权限管理模型。

Result: 在PC和手机上的三个真实应用评估显示，OAuthHub需要适度的代码修改且性能开销可忽略。18名开发者的研究表明，使用OAuthHub比传统OAuth API完成任务显著更快（9.1分钟 vs 18.0分钟），代码量更少（4.7行 vs 15.8行）。

Conclusion: OAuthHub通过利用个人设备作为中介控制器，提供了一种更精细、声明式的OAuth数据访问框架，有效减少了不必要的数据暴露，同时保持了开发效率和性能。

Abstract: Most OAuth service providers, such as Google and Microsoft, offer only a limited range of coarse-grained data access. As a result, third-party OAuth applications often end up accessing more user data than necessary, even if their developers want to minimize data access. We present OAuthHub, a development framework that leverages users' personal devices as the intermediary controller for OAuth-based data sharing between cloud services. The key innovations of OAuthHub are: (1) the insight that discretionary data access is largely unnecessary for most OAuth apps, which typically only require access at three well-defined moments-during installation, in response to user actions, and at scheduled intervals; (2) a development framework that requires explicit declarations of intended data access and supports the three common access patterns through intermittently available personal devices; and (3) a centralized runtime permission model for managing OAuth access across providers. We evaluated OAuthHub with three real-world apps on both PCs and mobile phones and found that OAuthHub requires moderate changes to the application code and imposes insignificant performance overheads. Our study with 18 developers showed that participants completed programming tasks significantly faster (9.1 vs. 18.0 minutes) with less code (4.7 vs. 15.8 lines) using OAuthHub than conventional OAuth APIs.

</details>


### [79] [ADVERSA: Measuring Multi-Turn Guardrail Degradation and Judge Reliability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10068)
*Harry Owiredu-Ashley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ADVERSA是一个自动化红队框架，用于评估LLM安全防护在持续对抗交互中的退化动态，而非仅测试单次提示的二元通过/失败结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全评估主要关注单次提示的二元通过/失败结果，无法捕捉在持续对抗交互中安全属性的演化动态。需要一种能够测量防护栏退化动态的连续评估框架。

Method: 使用微调的70B攻击模型(ADVERSA-Red，基于Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct的QLoRA微调)，消除攻击方安全拒绝问题；采用结构化5点评分标准，将部分合规视为可测量的状态；使用三重评判共识架构，将评判可靠性作为首要研究结果进行测量；在三个前沿受害者模型上进行控制实验。

Result: 在15个最多10轮对抗对话中，观察到26.7%的越狱率，平均越狱轮数为1.25轮，表明成功越狱集中在早期而非通过持续压力累积；记录了评判间一致性率、自我评判评分倾向、攻击模型漂移作为训练分布外部署的失效模式，以及攻击方拒绝作为受害者抵抗测量的先前未充分报告的混淆因素。

Conclusion: ADVERSA框架提供了测量LLM安全防护退化动态的连续评估方法，揭示了持续对抗交互中的安全演化模式，强调了攻击方拒绝作为测量混淆因素的重要性，并提出了攻击模型漂移等新的失效模式。

Abstract: Most adversarial evaluations of large language model (LLM) safety assess single prompts and report binary pass/fail outcomes, which fails to capture how safety properties evolve under sustained adversarial interaction. We present ADVERSA, an automated red-teaming framework that measures guardrail degradation dynamics as continuous per-round compliance trajectories rather than discrete jailbreak events. ADVERSA uses a fine-tuned 70B attacker model (ADVERSA-Red, Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct with QLoRA) that eliminates the attacker-side safety refusals that render off-the-shelf models unreliable as attackers, scoring victim responses on a structured 5-point rubric that treats partial compliance as a distinct measurable state.
  We report a controlled experiment across three frontier victim models (Claude Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.2) using a triple-judge consensus architecture in which judge reliability is measured as a first-class research outcome rather than assumed. Across 15 conversations of up to 10 adversarial rounds, we observe a 26.7% jailbreak rate with an average jailbreak round of 1.25, suggesting that in this evaluation setting, successful jailbreaks were concentrated in early rounds rather than accumulating through sustained pressure. We document inter-judge agreement rates, self-judge scoring tendencies, attacker drift as a failure mode in fine-tuned attackers deployed out of their training distribution, and attacker refusals as a previously-underreported confound in victim resistance measurement. All limitations are stated explicitly. Attack prompts are withheld per responsible disclosure policy; all other experimental artifacts are released.

</details>


### [80] [Building Privacy-and-Security-Focused Federated Learning Infrastructure for Global Multi-Centre Healthcare Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10063)
*Fan Zhang,Daniel Kreuter,Javier Fernandez-Marques,BloodCounts Consortium,Gregory Verghese,Bernard Butler,Nicholas Lane,Suthesh Sivapalaratnam,Joseph Taylor,Norbert C. J. de Wit,Nicholas S. Gleadall,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Michael Roberts*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FLA³：一种治理感知的联邦学习平台，通过运行时策略执行实现跨机构医疗AI协作，在保护数据隐私的同时确保监管合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机构医疗研究需要多样化临床数据，但跨境数据共享受隐私法规严格限制。现有联邦学习框架多为概念验证，缺乏对未授权参与、滥用和缺乏问责等治理风险的有效解决方案，特别是缺少可执行的认证、授权和计费机制，限制了临床实际部署。

Method: 提出FLA³平台，将监管义务通过运行时策略执行操作化。平台集成XACML兼容的基于属性的访问控制、加密计费和研究范围的联邦，直接嵌入联邦学习编排层，以强制执行机构主权和协议遵守。

Result: 通过两项互补研究进行评估：1) 在四个国家五个机构的BloodCounts!联盟中部署平台基础设施，证明操作可行性；2) 使用INTERVAL研究中25个中心35,315名受试者的54,446个全血计数样本进行模拟联邦，评估临床效用。结果显示FLA³在严格实施治理约束的同时，达到与集中训练相当的预测性能。

Conclusion: 可执行的治理可以作为一级隐私保护控制机制，提高跨司法管辖区医疗部署中可扩展AI的可信度，实现监管合规与AI效能的平衡。

Abstract: Collaborative healthcare research across multiple institutions increasingly requires diverse clinical datasets, but cross-border data sharing is strictly constrained by privacy regulations. Federated learning (FL) enables model training while keeping data local; however, many existing frameworks remain proof-of-concept and do not adequately address governance risks such as unauthorised participation, misuse, and lack of accountability. In particular, enforceable mechanisms for authentication, authorisation, and accounting (AAA) are often missing, limiting real-world clinical deployment. This paper presents FLA$^3$ (Federated Learning with Authentication, Authorisation, and Accounting), a governance-aware federated learning platform that operationalises regulatory obligations through runtime policy enforcement. FLA$^3$ integrates eXtensible Access Control Markup Language (XACML) compliant attribute-based access control (ABAC), cryptographic accounting, and study-scoped federation directly into the federated learning orchestration layer to enforce institutional sovereignty and protocol adherence. We evaluate FLA$^3$ through two complementary studies. First, we demonstrate operational feasibility by deploying the platform infrastructure across five BloodCounts! Consortium institutions in four countries: United Kingdom, Netherlands, India, and The Gambia. Second, we assess clinical utility using simulated federation of full blood count (FBC) data from 54,446 samples from 35,315 subjects across 25 centres in the INTERVAL study. Results show that FLA$^3$ achieves predictive performance comparable to centralised training while strictly enforcing governance constraints. These results show that enforceable governance can function as a first-class privacy-preserving control, improving trustworthiness for scalable artificial intelligence (AI) in cross-jurisdictional healthcare deployments.

</details>


### [81] [Why LLMs Fail: A Failure Analysis and Partial Success Measurement for Automated Security Patch Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10072)
*Amir Al-Maamari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究评估了大型语言模型在安全漏洞修复中的表现，发现仅有24.8%的补丁完全正确，51.4%同时存在安全和功能问题，主要失败原因是语义理解错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型在自动程序修复方面显示出潜力，但其在安全漏洞修复方面的有效性尚未得到充分评估。本研究旨在系统分析LLM生成的安全补丁在实际漏洞修复中的表现。

Method: 使用Vul4J基准测试中的64个Java漏洞，生成319个LLM安全补丁。采用三轴评估方法：编译能力、通过PoV测试的安全验证、通过测试套件的功能验证。提出安全修复评分(SRS)量化修复效果。

Result: 仅24.8%的补丁完全正确，51.4%同时存在安全和功能问题。主要失败模式是语义误解：LLM生成语法有效但修复策略错误的代码。SRS显示LLM保持功能能力（均值0.832）但安全修复能力差（均值0.251）。漏洞类型强烈预测修复难度，修复率从0%（输入验证）到45%（无限循环）不等。

Conclusion: LLM生成的安全补丁在部署前需要严格验证。虽然LLM在保持功能方面表现良好，但在安全修复方面存在显著缺陷，特别是对漏洞语义的理解不足。漏洞类型是修复成功率的重要预测因素。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for Automated Program Repair (APR), yet their effectiveness on security vulnerabilities remains poorly characterized. This study analyzes 319 LLM-generated security patchesacross 64 Java vulnerabilities from the Vul4J benchmark. Using tri-axis evaluation (compilation, security via PoV tests, functionality via test suites), the analysis reveals that only 24.8% of patches achieve full correctness, while 51.4% fail both security and functionality. The dominant failure mode is semantic misunderstanding: LLMs produce syntactically valid code but apply incorrect repair strategies. The proposed Security Repair Score (SRS) quantifies this gap, showing LLMs preserve functionality (mean 0.832) but struggle with security (mean 0.251). Vulnerability type strongly predicts difficulty, with fix rates ranging from 0% (input validation) to 45% (infinite loop). These findings demonstrate that LLM security patches require rigorous validation before deployment.

</details>


### [82] [TASER: Task-Aware Spectral Energy Refine for Backdoor Suppression in UAV Swarms Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10075)
*Sizhe Huang,Shujie Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TASER是一种针对无人机去中心化联邦学习中隐蔽后门攻击的防御框架，通过梯度谱分析而非异常检测，利用后门梯度特有的谱集中特性来抑制后门任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于异常检测的防御方法在无人机去中心化联邦学习环境中面临挑战：缺乏全局协调、资源有限，且容易被精心设计的隐蔽后门攻击绕过。研究发现攻击者越努力模仿良性行为，其梯度的谱集中特性越明显，这为谱分析防御提供了理论基础。

Method: 提出任务感知谱能量精炼（TASER）框架：1）通过梯度谱分析识别后门梯度的谱集中特性；2）保留与主任务相关的频率系数，丢弃其他系数，从而在结构上破坏后门任务；3）提供理论保证，证明该方法能有效抑制后门任务。

Result: 实验表明TASER能有效防御那些能绕过基于异常检测防御的隐蔽后门攻击，将攻击成功率降至20%以下，同时主任务准确率损失控制在5%以内。

Conclusion: TASER是首个利用谱集中特性而非复杂异常检测的高效后门防御框架，为无人机去中心化联邦学习中的隐蔽后门攻击防御提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As backdoor attacks in UAV-based decentralized federated learning (DFL) grow increasingly stealthy and sophisticated, existing defenses have likewise escalated in complexity. Yet these defenses, which rely heavily on outlier detection, remain vulnerable to carefully crafted backdoors. In UAV-DFL, the lack of global coordination and limited resources further render outlier-based defenses impractical. Against this backdrop, gradient spectral analysis offers a promising alternative. While prior work primarily leverages low-frequency coefficients for pairwise comparisons, it neglects to analyze the intrinsic spectral characteristics of backdoor gradients. Through empirical analysis of existing stealthy attacks, we reveal a key insight: the more effort attackers invest in mimicking benign behaviors, the more distinct the spectral concentration becomes. Motivated by this, we propose Task-Aware Spectral Energy Refine (TASER) -- a decentralized defense framework. To our knowledge, this is the first efficient backdoor defense that utilizes spectral concentration instead of complex outlier detection, enabling mitigation of stealthy attacks by structurally disrupting the backdoor task. To suppress the backdoor task, TASER preserves main-task-relevant frequency coefficients and discards others. We provide theoretical guarantees and demonstrate through experiments that TASER remains effective against stealthy backdoor attacks that bypass outlier-based defenses, achieving attack success rate below 20% and accuracy loss under 5%.

</details>


### [83] [Amnesia: Adversarial Semantic Layer Specific Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10080)
*Ali Raza,Gurang Gupta,Nikolay Matyunin,Jibesh Patra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Amnesia是一种轻量级的激活空间对抗攻击，通过操纵Transformer内部状态来绕过开源大语言模型的安全机制，无需微调即可生成有害内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有技术（如基于人类反馈的强化学习）试图将LLM输出与人类价值观对齐，但这些措施是否足以防止生成有害内容仍不确定。需要验证现有安全机制的有效性，并揭示开源LLM的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出Amnesia攻击方法，这是一种轻量级的激活空间对抗攻击。通过操纵Transformer模型的内部状态（激活空间），直接绕过现有安全机制，无需对模型进行微调或额外训练。

Result: 在先进的开源LLM上进行实验表明，该攻击能有效规避现有安全防护，诱导模型生成各种反社会行为内容。在基准数据集上的实验证实了攻击的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了开源LLM需要更强大的安全措施，并强调了持续研究以防止潜在滥用的重要性。现有安全机制存在被绕过风险，需要开发更鲁棒的防护技术。

Abstract: Warning: This article includes red-teaming experiments, which contain examples of compromised LLM responses that may be offensive or upsetting.
  Large Language Models (LLMs) have the potential to create harmful content, such as generating sophisticated phishing emails and assisting in writing code of harmful computer viruses. Thus, it is crucial to ensure their safe and responsible response generation. To reduce the risk of generating harmful or irresponsible content, researchers have developed techniques such as reinforcement learning with human feedback to align LLM's outputs with human values and preferences. However, it is still undetermined whether such measures are sufficient to prevent LLMs from generating interesting responses. In this study, we propose Amnesia, a lightweight activation-space adversarial attack that manipulates internal transformer states to bypass existing safety mechanisms in open-weight LLMs. Through experimental analysis on state-of-the-art, open-weight LLMs, we demonstrate that our attack effectively circumvents existing safeguards, enabling the generation of harmful content without the need for any fine-tuning or additional training. Our experiments on benchmark datasets show that the proposed attack can induce various antisocial behaviors in LLMs. These findings highlight the urgent need for more robust security measures in open-weight LLMs and underscore the importance of continued research to prevent their potential misuse.

</details>


### [84] [Multi-Stream Perturbation Attack: Breaking Safety Alignment of Thinking LLMs Through Concurrent Task Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10091)
*Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出多流扰动攻击方法，通过交织多个任务流在单个提示中生成叠加干扰，针对LLM思维模式的独特漏洞进行攻击，在多个基准测试中取得高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM思维模式的广泛应用在增强复杂任务处理能力的同时引入了新的安全风险。思维模式在处理交织的多个任务时表现出独特漏洞，当受到越狱攻击时，逐步推理过程可能导致模型生成更详细的有害内容。

Method: 提出多流扰动攻击方法，通过交织多个任务流在单个提示中生成叠加干扰。设计三种扰动策略：多流交织（通过并发任务交织干扰思维过程）、反转扰动（字符反转）和形状变换（格式约束）。

Result: 在JailbreakBench、AdvBench和HarmBench数据集上，该方法在主流模型（包括Qwen3系列、DeepSeek、Qwen3-Max和Gemini 2.5 Flash）上的攻击成功率超过大多数方法。实验显示思维崩溃率和响应重复率分别达到17%和60%，表明多流扰动不仅能绕过安全机制，还能导致思维过程崩溃或重复输出。

Conclusion: 思维模式在处理交织多任务时存在独特安全漏洞，多流扰动攻击能有效利用这些漏洞，不仅提高攻击成功率，还能导致模型思维过程崩溃，揭示了LLM思维模式安全防护的新挑战。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of thinking mode in large language models (LLMs) has significantly enhanced complex task processing capabilities while introducing new security risks. When subjected to jailbreak attacks, the step-by-step reasoning process may cause models to generate more detailed harmful content. We observe that thinking mode exhibits unique vulnerabilities when processing interleaved multiple tasks. Based on this observation, we propose multi-stream perturbation attack, which generates superimposed interference by interweaving multiple task streams within a single prompt. We design three perturbation strategies: multi-stream interleaving, inversion perturbation, and shape transformation, which disrupt the thinking process through concurrent task interleaving, character reversal, and format constraints respectively. On JailbreakBench, AdvBench, and HarmBench datasets, our method achieves attack success rates exceeding most methods across mainstream models including Qwen3 series, DeepSeek, Qwen3-Max, and Gemini 2.5 Flash. Experiments show thinking collapse rates and response repetition rates reach up to 17% and 60% respectively, indicating multi-stream perturbation not only bypasses safety mechanisms but also causes thinking process collapse or repetitive outputs.

</details>


### [85] [Detecting Privilege Escalation with Temporal Braid Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10094)
*Christophe Parisel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 使用Burau Lyapunov指数作为代数探针，在云权限图的时间演化中识别两种风险机制边界，证明阿贝尔统计量无法确定该指数，利用非交换优势自动化风险权限流分类和治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云权限图的时间演化过程中，需要有效区分两种不同的风险机制（分散式和集中式），以便自动化分类和治理风险权限流。传统阿贝尔统计量（边计数、净权限流、门触发率）无法准确识别风险边界。

Method: 在强连通分量(SCCs)中使用Burau Lyapunov指数作为代数探针，证明该指数无法由任何阿贝尔统计量确定，利用非交换代数特性来区分两种风险机制。

Result: 发现非交换优势虽然较小但具有可操作性，能够有效区分分散式和集中式两种风险机制，为自动化分类和治理风险云权限流提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: Burau Lyapunov指数作为非交换代数探针，能够识别云权限流风险机制的边界，为自动化风险分类和治理提供了新的数学工具，弥补了传统阿贝尔统计量的不足。

Abstract: Within the Strongly Connected Components (SCCs) formed during the temporal evolution of a Cloud permission graph, we use the Burau Lyapunov exponent LE as an algebraic probe to locate the boundary between two risks regimes. We prove that no Abelian statistic (edge counts, net privilege flow, gate-firing rates) can determine LE. The non-commutation advantage is small, but actionable: we show how to leverage it to discriminate the two outstanding risk regimes, that we call dispersed and focused, for automating classification and governing remediation of risky Cloud permission flows.

</details>


### [86] [ACE Runtime - A ZKP-Native Blockchain Runtime with Sub-Second Cryptographic Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10242)
*Jian Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ACE Runtime是一种基于身份授权分离的ZKP原生执行层，用轻量级HMAC认证替代每笔交易的签名验证，通过异步证明阶段生成每块的聚合零知识最终性证书，实现亚秒级密码学最终性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能区块链在关键路径上为每笔交易验证一个签名，导致O(N)验证成本、高硬件压力和困难的抗量子迁移问题。需要一种更高效的验证架构。

Method: 采用身份授权分离架构，构建认证-执行-证明流水线：热路径使用轻量级HMAC认证，异步阶段生成聚合零知识最终性证书，实现双层最终性（BFT投票的软最终性和证明验证的硬最终性）。

Result: 原型显示低CPU编排开销，模型分析预测每块验证成本恒定，非构建者验证器硬件要求降低，带宽效率优于每交易签名设计，支持亚秒级密码学最终性。

Conclusion: 身份授权分离是实用的亚秒级密码学最终性架构，为更强的抗量子组件提供了清晰路径，解决了现有区块链的验证瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Existing high performance blockchains verify one signature per transaction on the critical path, which creates O(N) verification cost, high hardware pressure, and difficult post quantum migration. This paper presents ACE Runtime, a ZKP native execution layer built on identity authorization separation. We replace per transaction signature checks with lightweight HMAC attestations in the hot path, then generate one aggregated zero knowledge finality certificate per block in an asynchronous prove stage. The system is organized as an Attest Execute Prove pipeline with two tier finality: soft finality from BFT voting and hard finality from proof verification. Under standard cryptographic assumptions, we provide formal arguments for attestation unforgeability and hard finality irreversibility. We also define a two phase timeout and backup proving path with witness availability gossip for liveness under builder failure. Quantitative results combine analytical modeling with reference implementation measurements. The prototype shows low CPU orchestration overhead, while model driven analysis projects constant per block verification cost, lower validator hardware requirements for non builders, and better bandwidth efficiency than per transaction signature designs. These results indicate that identity authorization separation is a practical architecture for sub second cryptographic finality with a clear path toward stronger post quantum components.

</details>


### [87] [Post-Quantum Entropy as a Service for Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10274)
*Javier Blanco-Romero,Yuri Melissa Garcia-Niño,Florina Almenares Mendoza,Daniel Díaz-Sánchez,Carlos García-Rubio,Celeste Campo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 构建量子熵即服务系统，将量子随机数生成器熵源通过后量子安全通道传输到ESP32客户端，实现比传统加密更快的后量子密钥交换和认证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式设备缺乏高质量熵源且难以承受重量级协议，需要为小型设备提供可信的量子熵服务

Method: 设计QEaaS系统：服务器端通过自定义OpenSSL提供直接量子熵和Linux系统池混合熵；客户端扩展libcoap的Zephyr支持，集成wolfSSL-based DTLS 1.3到CoAP栈，添加BLAKE2s熵池并保留标准接口；使用ML-KEM-512和ML-DSA-44等后量子算法

Result: 在ESP32硬件上，ML-KEM-512完成DTLS 1.3握手平均313ms（无证书验证），比ECDHE P-256快35%；ML-KEM-512+ML-DSA-44组合平均225ms；证书验证仅增加17ms（ML-DSA-44）vs 194ms（ECDSA）；完全后量子配置比传统ECDHE P-256+ECDSA快63%；本地BLAKE2s池操作低于0.1ms

Conclusion: 在该平台上，后量子密钥交换和认证不仅可行，而且比传统基线更快，为嵌入式设备提供了实用的量子熵服务解决方案

Abstract: Embedded cryptography stands or falls on entropy quality, yet small devices have few trustworthy sources and little tolerance for heavyweight protocols. We build a Quantum Entropy as a Service (QEaaS) system that moves QRNG-derived entropy from a Quantis device to ESP32-class clients over post-quantum-secured channels. On the server side, the design exposes two paths: direct quantum entropy through a custom OpenSSL provider and mixed entropy through the Linux system pool. On the client side, we extend libcoap's Zephyr support, integrate wolfSSL-based DTLS 1.3 into the CoAP stack, and add a BLAKE2s entropy pool that preserves the standard Zephyr extraction interface while introducing an injection API for server-provided entropy. Benchmarks on ESP32 hardware, targeting 100 iterations per configuration, show that ML-KEM-512 completes a DTLS 1.3 handshake in 313 ms on average without certificate verification, 35% faster than ECDHE P-256. Pairing ML-KEM-512 with ML-DSA-44 lowers the mean to 225 ms. Certificate verification adds roughly 194 ms for ECDSA but only 17 ms for ML-DSA-44, so the fully post-quantum configuration remains 63% faster than classical ECDHE P-256 with ECDSA even under full verification. Local BLAKE2s pool operations stay below 0.1 ms combined. On this platform, post-quantum key exchange and authentication are not only feasible; they are faster than the classical baseline.

</details>


### [88] [PRoADS: Provably Secure and Robust Audio Diffusion Steganography with latent optimization and backward Euler Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10314)
*YongPeng Yan,Yanan Li,Qiyang Xiao,Yanzhen Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PRoADS是一个基于音频扩散模型的可证明安全鲁棒音频隐写框架，通过正交矩阵投影将秘密信息嵌入扩散模型的初始噪声中，采用潜在优化和后向欧拉反演降低重构误差，在64kbps MP3压缩下BER仅为0.15%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频隐写方法在鲁棒性和安全性方面存在不足，特别是扩散模型反演过程中的重构误差导致高误码率，需要设计能够抵抗音频压缩等攻击的鲁棒隐写方案

Method: 1. 基于音频扩散模型的生成式隐写框架；2. 通过正交矩阵投影将秘密信息嵌入扩散模型的初始噪声；3. 引入潜在优化和后向欧拉反演技术最小化潜在重构和扩散反演误差

Result: 在64kbps MP3压缩下实现0.15%的极低误码率，显著优于现有方法，展现出强大的鲁棒性

Conclusion: PRoADS是一个可证明安全且鲁棒的音频隐写框架，通过创新的正交矩阵投影和误差最小化技术，在保持音频质量的同时实现了高鲁棒性和低误码率

Abstract: This paper proposes PRoADS, a provably secure and robust audio steganographic framework based on audio diffusion models. As a generative steganography scheme, PRoADS embeds secret messages into the initial noise of diffusion models via orthogonal matrix projection. To address the reconstruction errors in diffusion inversion that cause high bit error rates (BER), we introduce Latent Optimization and Backward Euler Inversion to minimize the latent reconstruction and diffusion inversion errors. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our scheme sustains a remarkably low BER of 0.15\% under 64 kbps MP3 compression, significantly outperforming existing methods and exhibiting strong robustness.

</details>


### [89] [The Orthogonal Vulnerabilities of Generative AI Watermarks: A Comparative Empirical Benchmark of Spatial and Latent Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10323)
*Jesse Yu,Nicholas Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过对抗性攻击模拟引擎，系统评估了空间域（RivaGAN）和潜在域（Tree-Ring）两种主流不可见水印方案，发现它们在现代生成式AI编辑工具面前存在相互正交的脆弱性，证明单域水印技术已不足以应对当前威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源生成式AI的快速普及，超逼真媒体合成能力对数字信任构成严峻挑战。现有不可见水印技术主要分为空间域和潜在域两种数学流形，但缺乏针对现代生成式AI编辑工具的严格比较基准测试。

Method: 使用自动化攻击模拟引擎，在30个强度区间内对两种代表性方案（空间域的RivaGAN和潜在域的Tree-Ring）进行几何和生成式扰动测试。提出"对抗性规避区域"（AER）框架，测量密码学退化与语义视觉保留（OpenCLIP > 70.0）的关系，每个区间进行100次实验。

Result: 统计分析显示两种水印域存在相互正交的脆弱性：空间域水印在算法像素重写（如图像到图像转换）下遭受严重密码学退化（AER规避率达67.47%），而潜在域水印对几何错位（如静态裁剪）表现出显著脆弱性（AER规避率达43.20%）。

Conclusion: 单域水印技术在现代对抗性工具集面前存在根本性不足，这暴露了当前数字溯源标准的系统性漏洞，为未来多域密码学架构的研发奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: As open-weights generative AI rapidly proliferates, the ability to synthesize hyper-realistic media has introduced profound challenges to digital trust. Automated disinformation and AI-generated imagery have made robust digital provenance a critical cybersecurity imperative. Currently, state-of-the-art invisible watermarks operate within one of two primary mathematical manifolds: the spatial domain (post-generation pixel embedding) or the latent domain (pre-generation frequency embedding). While existing literature frequently evaluates these models against isolated, classical distortions, there is a critical lack of rigorous, comparative benchmarking against modern generative AI editing tools. In this study, we empirically evaluate two leading representative paradigms, RivaGAN (Spatial) and Tree-Ring (Latent), utilizing an automated Attack Simulation Engine across 30 intensity intervals of geometric and generative perturbations. We formalize an "Adversarial Evasion Region" (AER) framework to measure cryptographic degradation against semantic visual retention (OpenCLIP > 70.0). Our statistical analysis ($n=100$ per interval, $MOE = \pm 3.92\%$) reveals that these domains possess mutually exclusive, mathematically orthogonal vulnerabilities. Spatial watermarks experience severe cryptographic degradation under algorithmic pixel-rewriting (exhibiting a 67.47% AER evasion rate under Img2Img translation), whereas latent watermarks exhibit profound fragility against geometric misalignment (yielding a 43.20% AER evasion rate under static cropping). By proving that single-domain watermarking is fundamentally insufficient against modern adversarial toolsets, this research exposes a systemic vulnerability in current digital provenance standards and establishes the foundational exigence for future multi-domain cryptographic architectures.

</details>


### [90] [Naïve Exposure of Generative AI Capabilities Undermines Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10504)
*Sunpill Kim,Chanwoo Hwang,Minsu Kim,Jae Hong Seo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 生成式AI系统通过聊天机器人界面暴露的强大推理和图像精修能力，从根本上破坏了现代深度伪造检测器。研究发现，仅使用合规提示和商业AI系统就能让精修图像同时逃过检测、保持身份识别并提高感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成式AI系统暴露的强大推理和图像精修能力如何破坏深度伪造检测器。关注现实部署场景：攻击者仅使用良性、合规的提示和商业生成式AI系统，而非提出新的图像操纵技术。

Method: 通过商业生成式AI系统的聊天机器人界面，利用其明确表达的认证标准和不受限制的推理能力，将这些标准直接复用为精修目标。研究语义保持的图像精修对最先进深度伪造检测方法的影响。

Result: 精修后的图像能同时逃过检测（使现有检测方法失效）、保持身份识别（商业人脸识别API验证）、并显著提高感知质量。商业聊天机器人服务比开源模型构成更大安全风险，因其更高的真实感、语义可控性和低门槛界面使非专家用户也能有效实施规避。

Conclusion: 当前检测框架假设的威胁模型与真实世界生成式AI的实际能力存在结构性不匹配。检测基线主要受先前基准测试影响，而部署的系统尽管在其他领域有严格安全控制，却暴露了不受限制的认证推理和精修能力。

Abstract: Generative AI systems increasingly expose powerful reasoning and image refinement capabilities through user-facing chatbot interfaces. In this work, we show that the naïve exposure of such capabilities fundamentally undermines modern deepfake detectors. Rather than proposing a new image manipulation technique, we study a realistic and already-deployed usage scenario in which an adversary uses only benign, policy-compliant prompts and commercial generative AI systems. We demonstrate that state-of-the-art deepfake detection methods fail under semantic-preserving image refinement. Specifically, we show that generative AI systems articulate explicit authenticity criteria and inadvertently externalize them through unrestricted reasoning, enabling their direct reuse as refinement objectives. As a result, refined images simultaneously evade detection, preserve identity as verified by commercial face recognition APIs, and exhibit substantially higher perceptual quality. Importantly, we find that widely accessible commercial chatbot services pose a significantly greater security risk than open-source models, as their superior realism, semantic controllability, and low-barrier interfaces enable effective evasion by non-expert users. Our findings reveal a structural mismatch between the threat models assumed by current detection frameworks and the actual capabilities of real-world generative AI. While detection baselines are largely shaped by prior benchmarks, deployed systems expose unrestricted authenticity reasoning and refinement despite stringent safety controls in other domains.

</details>


### [91] [An Approach for Safe and Secure Software Protection Supported by Symbolic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10608)
*Daniel Dorfmeister,Flavio Ferrarotti,Bernhard Fischer,Evelyn Haslinger,Rudolf Ramler,Markus Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于物理不可克隆函数(PUF)的工业控制软件防拷贝方法，确保软件只能在目标硬件上正确执行，并利用符号执行保证安全属性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制软件需要防止非法拷贝和逆向工程，传统保护方法存在安全漏洞，需要一种能确保软件与特定硬件绑定的可靠保护机制

Method: 基于物理不可克隆函数(PUF)实现硬件-软件绑定，利用符号执行技术验证安全属性，确保软件在非目标硬件上执行时仍保持安全行为

Result: 开发出能有效防止软件非法拷贝的保护方法，确保软件只能在目标硬件上正确执行，同时能抵抗逆向工程攻击

Conclusion: 基于PUF和符号执行的保护方法为工业控制软件提供了有效的防拷贝和防逆向工程解决方案，同时保证了安全属性的完整性

Abstract: We introduce a novel copy-protection method for industrial control software. With our method, a program executes correctly only on its target hardware and behaves differently on other machines. The hardware-software binding is based on Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs). We use symbolic execution to guarantee the preservation of safety properties if the software is executed on a different machine, or if there is a problem with the PUF response. Moreover, we show that the protection method is also secure against reverse engineering.

</details>


### [92] [Repurposing Backdoors for Good: Ephemeral Intrinsic Proofs for Verifiable Aggregation in Cross-silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10692)
*Xian Qin,Xue Yang,Xiaohu Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级可验证聚合架构，利用后门注入在模型参数中嵌入验证信号，通过灾难性遗忘实现即时验证与信号衰减，相比密码学方法实现1000倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全聚合保护联邦学习更新机密性但无法保证聚合完整性，恶意服务器可静默省略或篡改更新；现有可验证聚合方案依赖重量级密码学（如零知识证明、同态加密），计算成本随模型规模增长而急剧增加

Method: 提出从外部密码学证明转向内在证明的轻量级架构，重新利用后门注入将验证信号直接嵌入模型参数；利用灾难性遗忘使信号既鲁棒用于即时验证又短暂自然衰减以保持最终模型效用；设计随机单验证器审计框架，兼容安全聚合，确保客户端匿名性并防止信号冲突，无需可信第三方

Result: 在SVHN、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上的实验表明对恶意服务器具有高检测概率；在ResNet-18上相比密码学基线实现超过1000倍加速，能有效扩展到大型模型

Conclusion: 提出了一种创新的轻量级可验证聚合方法，通过内在证明机制解决了联邦学习中聚合完整性问题，在保持安全聚合优势的同时显著降低了计算开销，为大规模模型部署提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: While Secure Aggregation (SA) protects update confidentiality in Cross-silo Federated Learning, it fails to guarantee aggregation integrity, allowing malicious servers to silently omit or tamper with updates. Existing verifiable aggregation schemes rely on heavyweight cryptography (e.g., ZKPs, HE), incurring computational costs that scale poorly with model size. In this paper, we propose a lightweight architecture that shifts from extrinsic cryptographic proofs to \textit{Intrinsic Proofs}. We repurpose backdoor injection to embed verification signals directly into model parameters. By harnessing Catastrophic Forgetting, these signals are robust for immediate verification yet ephemeral, naturally decaying to preserve final model utility. We design a randomized, single-verifier auditing framework compatible with SA, ensuring client anonymity and preventing signal collision without trusted third parties. Experiments on SVHN, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 demonstrate high detection probabilities against malicious servers. Notably, our approach achieves over $1000\times$ speedup on ResNet-18 compared to cryptographic baselines, effectively scaling to large models.

</details>


### [93] [CacheSolidarity: Preventing Prefix Caching Side Channels in Multi-tenant LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10726)
*Panagiotis Georgios Pennas,Konstantinos Papaioannou,Marco Guarnieri,Thaleia Dimitra Doudali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CacheSolidarity系统通过智能监控缓存共享并选择性隔离前缀，在保护多租户LLM服务系统免受自动前缀缓存侧信道攻击的同时，保持高性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动前缀缓存(APC)虽然能加速LLM推理，但会引入时序侧信道攻击风险：缓存命中比未命中更快，攻击者可以利用这种延迟差异推断敏感信息。现有防御方法过于粗暴，通过禁用APC和缓存共享来隔离用户，牺牲了正常用户的效率。

Method: CacheSolidarity系统监控跨用户的缓存重用，标记可疑的共享模式，并选择性隔离前缀，仅在必要时限制其重用。系统采用轻量级设计，在保持安全性的同时最大化缓存效率。

Result: 评估显示，与现有隔离用户的防御方法相比，CacheSolidarity能够实现高达70%的更高缓存重用率和30%更低的推理延迟。系统在安全性和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: CacheSolidarity证明了LLM服务系统的安全性不必以不必要的性能降低或难以承受的开销为代价。通过智能监控和选择性隔离，可以在保持高性能的同时有效防御APC侧信道攻击。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on optimizations like Automatic Prefix Caching (APC) to accelerate inference. APC works by reusing previously computed states for the beginning part of a request (prefix), when another request starts with the same text. While APC improves throughput, it introduces timing side channels: cache hits are faster than misses, creating observable latency differences. In multi-tenant systems, attackers can exploit these differences to infer sensitive information, e.g., by incrementally reconstructing another user's request by observing hit/miss patterns. Current defenses take a sledgehammer approach: they disable APC and cache sharing, isolating users, and sacrificing efficiency for regular users. This paper presents CacheSolidarity, a system that secures multi-tenant LLM serving systems against APC side channels without sacrificing performance and efficiency. CacheSolidarity monitors cache reuse across users, flags suspicious sharing, and selectively isolates prefixes, restricting their reuse only when necessary. Evaluation shows that CacheSolidarity enables up to 70% higher cache reuse and 30% lower inference latency compared to existing defenses that isolate users. CacheSolidarity's lightweight design demonstrates how security in LLM serving does not have to come at the cost of unnecessarily reduced performance or unbearable overheads.

</details>


### [94] [MAD: Memory Allocation meets Software Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10840)
*Manuel Wiesinger,Daniel Dorfmeister,Stefan Brunthaler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAD系统通过内存分配多样性技术对抗RowHammer攻击，利用两种新颖的空间多样化技术克服内存子系统熵不足的问题，旨在延迟攻击并为其他响应措施创造时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM错误导致的漏洞（特别是RowHammer攻击）是一个尚未解决的棘手问题。现有防御措施针对早期RowHammer攻击的具体细节设计，无法适应近期攻击的泛化形式，且不清楚现有技术能否应对这些泛化攻击，可能需要全新的方法。

Method: 提出MAD（内存分配多样性）系统，结合内存分配与软件多样性原则，采用两种新颖的互补空间多样化技术来克服内存子系统熵不足的障碍。系统实现简单、性能影响可忽略，且硬件和软件无关。

Result: 早期结果显示该方法前景良好。从安全角度看，MAD的目标是通过最大程度延迟RowHammer攻击来阻止它们，为主动重启或对持续攻击的深入分析等额外响应措施创造时间窗口。

Conclusion: 尽管仍是进行中的工作，但MAD提供了一种结合内存分配和软件多样性原则的新方法，能够有效应对RowHammer攻击的泛化形式，具有实现简单、性能影响小、硬件软件无关等优势。

Abstract: Vulnerabilities emanating from DRAM errors pose a vexing problem that remains, as of yet, unsolved and elusive but cannot be ignored. Prior defenses focused on specific details of early RowHammer attacks and fail to generalize with the generalizations of recent RowHammer attacks. Even worse, it is presently not clear that techniques from prior defenses will be able to cope with these generalizations or if an entirely new approach is required. Although still work-in-progress, we have identified a new approach that combines memory allocation with principles underlying software diversity and shows promising early results. At first glance, software diversity seems to be an unlikely contender, since it faces seemingly insurmountable obstacles, primarily the lack of sufficient entropy in memory subsystems. Our system - called MAD, short for memory allocation diversity - leverages two novel, complementary spatial diversification techniques to overcome this entropy obstacle. Entropy aside, MAD offers ease-of-implementation, negligible performance impact, and is both hardware and software agnostic. From a security perspective, MAD's goal is to deter RowHammer attacks by delaying them to the maximum extent possible. Such a delay opens the door for a variety of additional responses, e.g., proactive rebooting, or complementary in-depth analysis of ongoing attacks that would be too slow for an always-on defense.

</details>


### [95] [Separating Oblivious and Adaptive Differential Privacy under Continual Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11029)
*Mark Bun,Marco Gaboardi,Connor Wagaman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Jain等人提出的开放问题，首次展示了持续观察模型中遗忘设置与自适应设置之间的显式分离，基于Bun等人的相关向量查询问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Jain等人提出的开放问题：在持续观察（流式算法）的差分隐私模型中，区分遗忘设置（数据流预先固定）和自适应设置（数据流可根据算法输出自适应选择）的隐私保护能力差异

Method: 基于Bun等人的相关向量查询问题构建分离实例。在遗忘设置中设计(ε,0)-DP算法，能在输入维度上保持指数级时间步长的准确性；在自适应设置中证明所有(ε,δ)-DP算法在常数时间步后都会失效

Result: 首次显式分离了持续观察模型中遗忘与自适应设置的隐私保护能力：遗忘设置支持指数级时间步长的准确算法，而自适应设置中任何算法在常数时间步后都会失效

Conclusion: 持续观察模型的差分隐私保护能力确实依赖于数据流的选择方式，遗忘设置与自适应设置存在本质差异，这为流式隐私算法的设计提供了重要理论边界

Abstract: We resolve an open question of Jain, Raskhodnikova, Sivakumar, and Smith (ICML 2023) by exhibiting a problem separating differential privacy under continual observation in the oblivious and adaptive settings. The continual observation (a.k.a. continual release) model formalizes privacy for streaming algorithms, where data is received over time and output is released at each time step. In the oblivious setting, privacy need only hold for data streams fixed in advance; in the adaptive setting, privacy is required even for streams that can be chosen adaptively based on the streaming algorithm's output.
  We describe the first explicit separation between the oblivious and adaptive settings. The problem showing this separation is based on the correlated vector queries problem of Bun, Steinke, and Ullman (SODA 2017). Specifically, we present an $(\varepsilon,0)$-DP algorithm for the oblivious setting that remains accurate for exponentially many time steps in the dimension of the input. On the other hand, we show that every $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP adaptive algorithm fails to be accurate after releasing output for only a constant number of time steps.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [A Diffusion Analysis of Policy Gradient for Stochastic Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10219)
*Tor Lattimore*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文研究了k臂随机赌博机的策略梯度连续时间扩散近似，证明了在特定学习率下的遗憾界，并构造了反例说明学习率过大的危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究策略梯度方法在随机赌博机问题中的理论性能，特别是通过连续时间扩散近似来分析其收敛性和遗憾界，为深度强化学习中的策略梯度方法提供理论支撑。

Method: 采用连续时间扩散近似来分析k臂随机赌博机的策略梯度方法，通过理论分析推导遗憾界，并构造特定实例来验证学习率条件的必要性。

Result: 当学习率η=O(Δ²/log(n))时，遗憾界为O(k log(k) log(n)/η)，其中n是时间步数，Δ是最小奖励差距。同时构造了一个只有对数数量臂的实例，证明除非η=O(Δ²)，否则遗憾是线性的。

Conclusion: 策略梯度方法在随机赌博机问题中需要适当的学习率设置（η=O(Δ²/log(n))）才能获得次线性遗憾，否则即使在臂数很少的情况下也可能产生线性遗憾。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time diffusion approximation of policy gradient for $k$-armed stochastic bandits. We prove that with a learning rate $η= O(Δ^2/\log(n))$ the regret is $O(k \log(k) \log(n) / η)$ where $n$ is the horizon and $Δ$ the minimum gap. Moreover, we construct an instance with only logarithmically many arms for which the regret is linear unless $η= O(Δ^2)$.

</details>


### [97] [Stability and Robustness via Regularization: Bandit Inference via Regularized Stochastic Mirror Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10184)
*Budhaditya Halder,Ishan Sengupta,Koustav Chowdhury,Koulik Khamaru*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为基于随机镜像下降的赌博机算法建立了系统稳定性理论，提出了正则化EXP3算法族，证明了稳定性与学习效率的兼容性，并展示了算法对对抗性破坏的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赌博机数据中的统计推断面临适应性采样的根本挑战，这违反了经典渐近理论的独立性假设。稳定性被确定为适应性下有效推断的充分条件，但需要为基于随机镜像下降的广泛算法框架建立系统稳定性理论。

Method: 1. 建立了基于随机镜像下降算法的通用稳定性准则：如果平均迭代以比率收敛到非随机概率向量，则诱导的赌博机算法是稳定的。2. 引入了使用对数障碍正则化器的正则化-EXP3算法族，并适当调整参数。3. 证明了这些算法满足稳定性准则，并建立了对线性泛函的Wald型置信区间。4. 开发了对抗性破坏下的鲁棒变体。

Result: 1. 正则化-EXP3算法满足稳定性准则，为均值参数的线性泛函提供名义覆盖的Wald型置信区间。2. 这些算法在忽略对数因子的情况下达到极小极大最优遗憾保证，表明稳定性与学习效率兼容。3. 改进的正则化-EXP3变体在存在o(T^{1/2})对抗性破坏时仍保持经验臂均值的渐近正态性，而UCB等稳定算法在仅对数级破坏下就会遭受线性遗憾。

Conclusion: 该论文为基于随机镜像下降的赌博机算法建立了系统稳定性理论框架，证明了稳定性与学习效率在镜像下降框架内是兼容的目标。正则化-EXP3算法族同时实现了有效推断和最优学习性能，并展现出对对抗性破坏的显著鲁棒性，为适应性采样下的统计推断提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Statistical inference with bandit data presents fundamental challenges due to adaptive sampling, which violates the independence assumptions underlying classical asymptotic theory. Recent work has identified stability as a sufficient condition for valid inference under adaptivity. This paper develops a systematic theory of stability for bandit algorithms based on stochastic mirror descent, a broad algorithmic framework that includes the widely-used EXP3 algorithm as a special case.
  Our contributions are threefold. First, we establish a general stability criterion: if the average iterates of a stochastic mirror descent algorithm converge in ratio to a non-random probability vector, then the induced bandit algorithm is stable. This result provides a unified lens for analyzing stability across diverse algorithmic instantiations. Second, we introduce a family of regularized-EXP3 algorithms employing a log-barrier regularizer with appropriately tuned parameters. We prove that these algorithms satisfy our stability criterion and, as an immediate corollary, that Wald-type confidence intervals for linear functionals of the mean parameter achieve nominal coverage. Notably, we show that the same algorithms attain minimax-optimal regret guarantees up to logarithmic factors, demonstrating that inference-enabling stability and learning efficiency are compatible objectives within the mirror descent framework. Third, we establish robustness to corruption: a modified variant of regularized-EXP3 maintains asymptotic normality of empirical arm means even in the presence of $o(T^{1/2})$ adversarial corruptions. This stands in sharp contrast to other stable algorithms such as UCB, which suffer linear regret even under logarithmic levels of corruption.

</details>


### [98] [Bayesian Hierarchical Models and the Maximum Entropy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10252)
*Brendon J. Brewer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 贝叶斯层次模型中，当给定超参数时先验为典型分布（具有矩约束的最大熵分布），则依赖的边际先验也具有最大熵性质，但约束条件不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯层次模型在实际数据分析中被广泛使用，通过引入超参数间接地为未知参数分配先验。这种模型产生的边际先验（对超参数积分后）通常是依赖的，使得学习一个参数能为其他参数提供信息。本文旨在阐明当给定超参数时先验为典型分布时，依赖的边际先验也具有最大熵性质，从而揭示分配层次模型时实际假设的信息。

Method: 作者采用理论分析方法，证明当给定超参数时先验是典型分布（即具有矩约束的最大熵分布）时，通过对超参数积分得到的依赖边际先验也具有最大熵性质。该方法基于最大熵原理，但约束条件变为未知量某个函数的边际分布约束。

Result: 研究结果表明，当层次模型中给定超参数时先验为典型分布时，依赖的边际先验也具有最大熵性质，但其约束条件不同于原始约束。新的约束是关于未知量某个函数的边际分布的约束。这一发现为理解层次模型中实际假设的信息提供了理论依据。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯层次模型中，当给定超参数时先验为典型分布，则依赖的边际先验也具有最大熵性质，但约束条件变为未知量函数的边际分布约束。这一结果阐明了分配层次模型时实际假设的信息内容，为层次模型的解释和应用提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Bayesian hierarchical models are frequently used in practical data analysis contexts. One interpretation of these models is that they provide an indirect way of assigning a prior for unknown parameters, through the introduction of hyperparameters. The resulting marginal prior for the parameters (integrating over the hyperparameters) is usually dependent, so that learning one parameter provides some information about the others. In this contribution, I will demonstrate that, when the prior given the hyperparameters is a canonical distribution (a maximum entropy distribution with moment constraints), the dependent marginal prior also has a maximum entropy property, with a different constraint. This constraint is on the marginal distribution of some function of the unknown quantities. The results shed light on what information is actually being assumed when we assign a hierarchical model.

</details>


### [99] [MultiwayPAM: Multiway Partitioning Around Medoids for LLM-as-a-Judge Score Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10287)
*Chihiro Watanabe,Jingyu Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出MultiwayPAM张量聚类方法，用于分析LLM-as-a-Judge评估框架中的评分偏差，通过同时估计数据张量各模态的聚类成员和中心点来揭示评估偏差结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge框架存在两个主要挑战：评估大量文本时的计算成本高昂，以及LLM评估器固有的偏差。需要揭示LLM评估器造成的评分偏差结构。

Method: 提出MultiwayPAM张量聚类方法，应用于LLM-as-a-Judge评分张量（包含问题、回答者、评估者组合的评分）。该方法能同时估计数据张量各模态的聚类成员和中心点。

Result: 通过MultiwayPAM分析两个实际数据集的评分张量，实验证明了该方法的有效性，能够揭示问题/回答者/评估者聚类的成员关系。

Conclusion: MultiwayPAM张量聚类方法能有效分析LLM-as-a-Judge评估框架中的评分偏差结构，为解决计算成本和评估偏差问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge is a flexible framework for text evaluation, which allows us to obtain scores for the quality of a given text from various perspectives by changing the prompt template. Two main challenges in using LLM-as-a-Judge are computational cost of LLM inference, especially when evaluating a large number of texts, and inherent bias of an LLM evaluator. To address these issues and reveal the structure of score bias caused by an LLM evaluator, we propose to apply a tensor clustering method to a given LLM-as-a-Judge score tensor, whose entries are the scores for different combinations of questions, answerers, and evaluators. Specifically, we develop a new tensor clustering method MultiwayPAM, with which we can simultaneously estimate the cluster membership and the medoids for each mode of a given data tensor. By observing the medoids obtained by MultiwayPAM, we can gain knowledge about the membership of each question/answerer/evaluator cluster. We experimentally show the effectiveness of MultiwayPAM by applying it to the score tensors for two practical datasets.

</details>


### [100] [On The Complexity of Best-Arm Identification in Non-Stationary Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10346)
*Leo Maynard-Zhang,Zhihan Xiong,Kevin Jamieson,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究非平稳线性赌博机中固定预算最优臂识别问题，提出基于邻接最优设计的算法，建立臂集依赖的复杂度下界并证明其紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统G-最优设计的下界过于悲观，因为它仅基于标准基向量臂集推导，未能充分利用具有丰富几何结构的臂集带来的潜在优势，需要建立适用于任意臂集的更精确下界。

Method: 提出邻接最优设计（Adjacent-optimal design），这是XY-最优设计的特化，并开发Adjacent-BAI算法。通过理论分析证明算法误差概率与下界匹配。

Result: 建立了适用于任意臂集的臂集依赖下界，证明了Adjacent-BAI算法的误差概率与该下界在常数因子内匹配，验证了下界的紧致性，确定了该设置的臂集依赖复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过邻接最优设计和相应算法，解决了非平稳线性赌博机中固定预算BAI问题的臂集依赖复杂度分析，为具有丰富几何结构的臂集提供了更精确的性能界限。

Abstract: We study the fixed-budget best-arm identification (BAI) problem in non-stationary linear bandits. Concretely, given a fixed time budget $T\in \mathbb{N}$, finite arm set $\mathcal{X} \subset \mathbb{R}^d$, and a potentially adversarial sequence of unknown parameters $\lbrace θ_t\rbrace_{t=1}^{T}$ (hence non-stationary), a learner aims to identify the arm with the largest cumulative reward $x_* = \arg\max_{x \in \mathcal{X}} x^\top\sum_{t=1}^T θ_t$ with high probability. In this setting, it is well-known that uniformly sampling arms from the G-optimal design yields a minimax-optimal error probability of $\exp\left(-Θ\left(T / H_{G}\right)\right)$, where $H_{G}$ scales proportionally with the dimension $d$. However, this notion of complexity is overly pessimistic, as it is derived from a lower bound in which the arm set consists only of the standard basis vectors, thus masking any potential advantages arising from arm sets with richer geometric structure. To address this, we establish an arm-set-dependent lower bound that, in contrast, holds for any arm set. Motivated by the ideas underlying our lower bound, we propose the Adjacent-optimal design, a specialization of the well-known $\mathcal{X}\mathcal{Y}$-optimal design, and develop the $\textsf{Adjacent-BAI}$ algorithm. We prove that the error probability of $\textsf{Adjacent-BAI}$ matches our lower bound up to constants, verifying the tightness of our lower bound, and establishing the arm-set-dependent complexity of this setting.

</details>


### [101] [Adaptive Active Learning for Regression via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10435)
*Simon D. Nguyen,Troy Russo,Kentaro Hoffman,Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出WiGS方法，用动态加性准则替代iGS的静态乘性规则，通过强化学习自适应调整探索-利用平衡，在回归任务中提高主动学习的标注效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有改进贪婪采样(iGS)方法使用静态乘性规则平衡特征空间多样性和输出空间不确定性，但在数据密度不规则区域会忽略高误差样本，需要更灵活的自适应策略。

Method: 提出加权改进贪婪采样(WiGS)，用动态加性准则替代静态乘性规则，将权重选择建模为强化学习问题，使智能体能在学习过程中自适应调整探索-利用平衡。

Result: 在18个基准数据集和合成环境上的实验表明，WiGS在准确性和标注效率方面均优于iGS和其他基线方法，特别是在数据密度不规则领域表现更佳。

Conclusion: WiGS通过动态加性准则和强化学习驱动的权重自适应，有效解决了iGS在数据密度不规则区域的局限性，显著提升了回归任务中主动学习的性能。

Abstract: Active learning for regression reduces labeling costs by selecting the most informative samples. Improved Greedy Sampling is a prominent method that balances feature-space diversity and output-space uncertainty using a static, multiplicative rule. We propose Weighted improved Greedy Sampling (WiGS), which replaces this framework with a dynamic, additive criterion. We formulate weight selection as a reinforcement learning problem, enabling an agent to adapt the exploration-investigation balance throughout learning. Experiments on 18 benchmark datasets and a synthetic environment show WiGS outperforms iGS and other baseline methods in both accuracy and labeling efficiency, particularly in domains with irregular data density where the baseline's multiplicative rule ignores high-error samples in dense regions.

</details>


### [102] [Brenier Isotonic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10452)
*Han Bao,Amirreza Eshraghi,Yutong Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Brenier保序回归，将经典保序回归扩展到多输出回归，通过最优传输的循环单调性约束，将回归函数解释为广义线性模型的连接函数，凸势函数解释为Brenier势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典保序回归仅适用于单变量非递减曲线，无法直接扩展到多输出回归问题。需要一种能够处理多输出回归中单调性约束的新方法，循环单调性为此提供了理论基础。

Method: 利用Kantorovich最优传输理论，将回归函数解释为广义线性模型的连接函数，凸势函数解释为Brenier势。通过最优传输的循环单调性耦合作为最优解，实现多输出回归的单调性约束。

Result: 在概率校准和广义线性模型实验中，Brenier保序回归表现优异，特别是在概率校准任务中，稳健地优于多个著名基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输理论将保序回归扩展到多输出回归问题，提出的Brenier保序回归方法在保持循环单调性约束的同时，在概率校准等应用中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Isotonic regression (IR) is shape-constrained regression to maintain a univariate fitting curve non-decreasing, which has numerous applications including single-index models and probability calibration. When it comes to multi-output regression, the classical IR is no longer applicable because the monotonicity is not readily extendable. We consider a novel multi-output regression problem where a regression function is \emph{cyclically monotone}. Roughly speaking, a cyclically monotone function is the gradient of some convex potential. Whereas enforcing cyclic monotonicity is apparently challenging, we leverage the fact that Kantorovich's optimal transport (OT) always yields a cyclically monotone coupling as an optimal solution. This perspective naturally allows us to interpret a regression function and the convex potential as a link function in generalized linear models and Brenier's potential in OT, respectively, and hence we call this IR extension \emph{Brenier isotonic regression}. We demonstrate experiments with probability calibration and generalized linear models. In particular, IR outperforms many famous baselines in probability calibration robustly.

</details>


### [103] [Dual Space Preconditioning for Gradient Descent in the Overparameterized Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10485)
*Reza Ghane,Danil Akhtiamov,Babak Hassibi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了双空间预条件梯度下降的收敛性，证明了在过参数化线性模型中，该方法总能收敛到满足数据拟合条件的解，并分析了不同预条件子的隐式偏差特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双空间预条件梯度下降（包括归一化梯度下降、梯度裁剪和Adam等优化器）的收敛性，特别关注在过参数化线性模型中的收敛行为和隐式偏差特性。

Method: 使用预条件子形式为∇K，其中K是凸函数，应用于训练过参数化线性模型ℓ(XW-Y)。引入新的Bregman散度版本及其恒等式来建立收敛性证明。

Result: 证明了预条件梯度下降的迭代总能收敛到满足XW∞=Y的点。对于各向同性预条件子，W∞最小化‖W∞-W₀‖²_F；对于一般预条件子，存在常数c使得‖W₀-W∞‖_F ≤ c‖W₀-W_GD,∞‖_F。

Conclusion: 双空间预条件梯度下降在过参数化线性模型中具有收敛保证，各向同性预条件子与标准梯度下降具有相同的隐式偏差，而一般预条件子的隐式偏差受限于常数倍的标准梯度下降解。

Abstract: In this work we study the convergence properties of the Dual Space Preconditioned Gradient Descent, encompassing optimizers such as Normalized Gradient Descent, Gradient Clipping and Adam. We consider preconditioners of the form $\nabla K$, where $K: \mathbb{R}^p \to \mathbb{R}$ is convex and assume that the latter is applied to train an over-parameterized linear model with loss of the form $\ell({X} {W} - {Y})$, for weights ${W} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times k}$, labels ${Y} \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times k}$ and data ${X} \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times d}$. Under the aforementioned assumptions, we prove that the iterates of the preconditioned gradient descent always converge to a point ${W}_{\infty} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times k}$ satisfying ${X}{W}_{\infty} = {Y}$. Our proof techniques are of independent interest as we introduce a novel version of the Bregman Divergence with accompanying identities that allow us to establish convergence. We also study the implicit bias of Dual Space Preconditioned Gradient Descent. First, we demonstrate empirically that, for general $K(\cdot)$, ${W}_\infty$ depends on the chosen learning rate, hindering a precise characterization of the implicit bias. Then, for preconditioners of the form $K({G}) = h(\|{G}\|_F)$, known as \textit{isotropic preconditioners}, we show that ${W}_\infty$ minimizes $\|{W}_\infty - {W}_0\|_F^2$ subject to ${X}{W}_\infty = {Y}$, where ${W}_0$ is the initialization. Denoting the convergence point of GD initialized at ${W}_0$ by ${W}_{\text{GD}, \infty}$, we thus note ${W}_{\infty} = {W}_{\text{GD}, \infty}$ for isotropic preconditioners. Finally, we show that a similar fact holds for general preconditioners up to a multiplicative constant, namely, $\|{W}_0 - {W}_{\infty}\|_F \le c \|{W}_0 - {W}_{\text{GD}, \infty}\|_F$ for a constant $c>0$.

</details>


### [104] [ReTabSyn: Realistic Tabular Data Synthesis via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10823)
*Xiaofeng Lin,Seungbae Kim,Zhuoya Li,Zachary DeSoto,Charles Fleming,Guang Cheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Deep generative models can help with data scarcity and privacy by producing synthetic training data, but they struggle in low-data, imbalanced tabular settings to fully learn the complex data distribution. We argue that striving for the full joint distribution could be overkill; for greater data efficiency, models should prioritize learning the conditional distribution $P(y\mid \bm{X})$, as suggested by recent theoretical analysis. Therefore, we overcome this limitation with \textbf{ReTabSyn}, a \textbf{Re}inforced \textbf{Tab}ular \textbf{Syn}thesis pipeline that provides direct feedback on feature correlation preservation during synthesizer training. This objective encourages the generator to prioritize the most useful predictive signals when training data is limited, thereby strengthening downstream model utility. We empirically fine-tune a language model-based generator using this approach, and across benchmarks with small sample sizes, class imbalance, and distribution shift, ReTabSyn consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Moreover, our approach can be readily extended to control various aspects of synthetic tabular data, such as applying expert-specified constraints on generated observations.

</details>


### [105] [Kernel Tests of Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10886)
*Xing Liu,Axel Gandy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于核的等价性检验方法，用于评估两个分布是否在统计意义上等价，克服传统拟合优度检验无法证明分布无差异的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拟合优度检验存在第二类错误问题，无法拒绝原假设可能只是检验功效不足，而非真正分布无差异。需要开发能够评估分布无统计显著差异的等价性检验方法。

Method: 使用两种核统计差异度量：核斯坦因差异和最大均值差异。原假设设定为候选分布与名义分布差异至少超过预定边界值。提出两种计算临界值的方法：渐近正态近似和自助法。

Result: 通过数值实验评估了所提检验方法的性能，验证了基于核的等价性检验在评估分布等价性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的核基等价性检验方法能够有效评估分布等价性，克服了传统方法的局限性，为统计实践中证明分布无显著差异提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We propose novel kernel-based tests for assessing the equivalence between distributions. Traditional goodness-of-fit testing is inappropriate for concluding the absence of distributional differences, because failure to reject the null hypothesis may simply be a result of lack of test power, also known as the Type-II error. This motivates \emph{equivalence testing}, which aims to assess the \emph{absence} of a statistically meaningful effect under controlled error rates. However, existing equivalence tests are either limited to parametric distributions or focus only on specific moments rather than the full distribution. We address these limitations using two kernel-based statistical discrepancies: the \emph{kernel Stein discrepancy} and the \emph{Maximum Mean Discrepancy}. The null hypothesis of our proposed tests assumes the candidate distribution differs from the nominal distribution by at least a pre-defined margin, which is measured by these discrepancies. We propose two approaches for computing the critical values of the tests, one using an asymptotic normality approximation, and another based on bootstrapping. Numerical experiments are conducted to assess the performance of these tests.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Universal Shuffle Asymptotics, Part II: Non-Gaussian Limits for Shuffle Privacy -- Poisson, Skellam, and Compound-Poisson Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10073)
*Alex Shvets*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文研究了随机洗牌实验中局部随机化器趋于集中时的临界行为，发现当经典Lindeberg条件失效时，洗牌分数会出现罕见的宏观跳跃，收敛到泊松/斯凯拉姆/复合泊松极限实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本系列第一部分（arXiv:2602.09029）在局部随机化器固定且具有远离零的全支撑时，建立了相邻洗牌实验的高斯极限理论。本文旨在刻画第一个普适性破缺前沿：当局部随机化器趋于集中时，经典Lindeberg条件失效，洗牌分数出现罕见的宏观跳跃，需要新的极限理论来描述这种临界行为。

Method: 针对洗牌二进制随机响应，在局部隐私参数ε₀(n)满足exp(ε₀(n))/n→c²的临界尺度下，证明了实验级收敛（Le Cam距离）到显式的平移极限实验：对典型相邻对得到泊松平移极限，对比例组合k/n→π∈(0,1)得到斯凯拉姆平移极限。对于一般有限字母表，引入稀疏误差临界机制，证明了中心化释放直方图的多变量复合泊松/独立泊松向量极限，得到多变量泊松平移实验和显式的极限(ε,δ)曲线。

Result: 建立了洗牌实验在临界尺度下的精确极限理论：1）二进制情况下，典型相邻对收敛到泊松平移极限，比例组合收敛到斯凯拉姆平移极限；2）一般有限字母表下，中心化释放直方图收敛到多变量复合泊松/独立泊松向量极限；3）给出了显式的极限(ε,δ)曲线作为多变量泊松级数。与第一部分结合，形成了高斯/GDP、临界泊松/斯凯拉姆/复合泊松、超临界无隐私的三机制图像。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了洗牌实验中局部随机化器趋于集中时的第一个普适性破缺前沿，建立了临界尺度下的泊松型极限理论，填补了高斯极限与无隐私机制之间的理论空白，为差分隐私洗牌机制的分析提供了完整的极限理论框架。

Abstract: Part I of this series (arXiv:2602.09029) develops a sharp Gaussian (LAN/GDP) limit theory for neighboring shuffle experiments when the local randomizer is fixed and has full support bounded away from zero. The present paper characterizes the first universality-breaking frontier: critical sequences of increasingly concentrated local randomizers for which classical Lindeberg conditions fail and the shuffle score exhibits rare macroscopic jumps.
  For shuffled binary randomized response with local privacy $\varepsilon_0 = \varepsilon_0(n)$, we prove experiment-level convergence (in Le Cam distance) to explicit shift limit experiments: a Poisson-shift limit for the canonical neighboring pair when $\exp(\varepsilon_0(n))/n \to c^2$, and a Skellam-shift limit for proportional compositions $k/n \to π\in (0,1)$ in the same scaling, including an explicit disappearance of the two-sided $δ$-floor away from boundary compositions.
  For general finite alphabets, we introduce a sparse-error critical regime and prove a multivariate compound-Poisson / independent Poisson vector limit for the centered released histogram, yielding a multivariate Poisson-shift experiment and an explicit limiting $(\varepsilon, δ)$ curve as a multivariate Poisson series. Together with Part I, these results yield a three-regime picture (Gaussian/GDP, critical Poisson/Skellam/compound-Poisson, and super-critical no privacy) under convergent macroscopic scalings.

</details>


### [107] [Methods for Identifying Minimal Sufficient Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10288)
*Rafael Oliveira Cavalcante,Alexandre Galvão Patriota*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文指出传统的最小充分统计量判别准则在一般情形下不成立，给出了基于Radon-Nikodym导数版本的反例，提出了版本鲁棒的判别方法，并将其扩展到非欧几里得空间，同时分析了Pfanzagl准则的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的最小充分统计量判别准则（Lehmann准则）在一般概率空间下可能失效，因为Radon-Nikodym导数的版本选择问题会影响准则的正确性。需要建立更稳健的判别方法，特别是在非欧几里得空间和标准Borel空间等更一般的统计设置中。

Method: 1. 给出基于Radon-Nikodym导数版本选择的反例，证明传统准则在一般情形下不成立；2. 提出版本鲁棒的判别准则，该准则在已知统计量充分性的前提下可直接验证；3. 将Sato(1996)的方法从欧几里得空间扩展到解析Borel样本空间和标准Borel统计空间；4. 分析Pfanzagl(1994,2017)提出的另一种判别准则，并提供离散反例说明其也需要额外假设。

Result: 1. 证明了传统最小充分统计量判别准则在一般概率空间下可能失效；2. 建立了版本鲁棒的判别方法，该方法在统计量已知充分时易于验证；3. 成功将Sato的方法推广到更一般的空间设置；4. 发现了Pfanzagl准则同样需要额外假设条件才能成立。

Conclusion: 传统的最小充分统计量判别准则存在版本依赖性问题，需要更稳健的判别方法。提出的版本鲁棒准则在已知充分性的前提下简单易用，且适用于更一般的概率空间。同时，Pfanzagl准则也需要类似的正则性条件。这些结果强调了在一般概率论框架下处理统计量充分性问题时需要注意技术细节。

Abstract: Let $f_θ$ be a joint density of a random sample $X$. A frequently used criterion asserts that a statistic $T(X)$ is minimal sufficient if, for any sample points $x$ and $y$, $T(x)=T(y)$ exactly when there exists a finite constant $h_{xy}>0$, independent of $θ$, such that $f_θ(y)=f_θ(x)h_{xy}$ for every $θ$. Although valid under regularity assumptions identified by Lehmann (1950), this statement is false as stated in general: we give a counterexample based on the choice of versions of Radon--Nikodym derivatives. We then propose a version-robust criterion that can be applied once sufficiency is established, and we use it to extend the method of Sato (1996) beyond Euclidean settings to analytic Borel sample spaces and standard Borel statistic spaces. While Lehmann--Scheffé--type regularity conditions and Sato's approach can be difficult to verify in practice, our method is straightforward to check when the statistic is known to be sufficient. Finally, we analyze a distinct criterion for identifying minimal sufficient statistics developed by (Pfanzagl,1994, Pfanzagl, 2017) and provide a discrete counterexample showing that it also requires additional assumptions.

</details>


### [108] [Strong Gaussian approximation for U-statistics in high dimensions and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10595)
*Weijia Li,Leheng Cai,Qirui Hu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文建立了高维非退化U统计量的强高斯逼近理论，为基于U统计量的高维推断提供了统一的概率理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据维度的不断增加，需要为高维U统计量建立可靠的理论基础，以支持基于U统计量的高维统计推断方法。

Method: 在充分丰富的概率空间上构造高斯过程来一致逼近整个序列U统计量过程，关键技术贡献包括完全退化U统计量的尖锐鞅极大不等式和高维独立和的强逼近。

Result: 获得了明确的逼近误差特征，该误差在维度多项式增长下消失；推导了U统计量基变点统计量的布朗桥逼近，并开发了具有完全枢轴极限分布的自标准化相关检验程序。

Conclusion: 该理论为基于U统计量的高维推断提供了统一的概率理论基础，能够处理有界核函数，因此在重尾分布下仍然有效。

Abstract: We establish a strong Gaussian approximation for high-dimensional non-degenerate U-statistics with diverging dimension. Under mild assumptions, we construct, on a sufficiently rich probability space, a Gaussian process that uniformly approximates the entire sequential U-statistic process. The approximation error is explicitly characterized and vanishes under polynomial growth of the dimension. The key technical contribution is a sharp martingale maximal inequality for completely degenerate U-statistics, combined with a high-dimensional strong approximation for independent sums. This coupling yields functional Gaussian limits without relying on $\mathcal{L}^\infty$-type bounds or bootstrap arguments. The theory is illustrated through three representative examples of U-statistics: the spatial Kendall's tau matrix, the multivariate Gini's mean difference, and the characteristic dispersion parameter. As applications, we derive Brownian bridge approximations for U-statistic-based change-point statistics and develop a self-normalized relevant testing procedure whose limiting distribution is fully pivotal. The framework naturally accommodates bounded kernels and therefore remains valid under heavy-tailed distributions. Overall, our results provide a unified probability-theoretic foundation for high-dimensional inference based on U-statistics.

</details>


### [109] [On positive definite thresholding of correlation matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11040)
*Sujit Sakharam Damase,James Eldred Pascoe*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究相关矩阵阈值化时保持正定性的方法，证明秩为n的矩阵进行软阈值化会导致特征空间几何坍缩，恢复信号能力受限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准相关矩阵阈值化技术会破坏正半定性，需要研究在特定集合上消失的正定函数构造，以确保阈值化后的矩阵仍是有效相关矩阵

Method: 构建在特定集合K⊆[-1,1)上消失的正定函数，基于归一化Gegenbauer展开的线性系数定义忠实性准则，类比编码理论中的Delsarte方法，提供单点和点对阈值化的界限

Result: 证明对于秩为n的相关矩阵，任何保持正半定性的软阈值化算子必然导致特征空间的几何坍缩，忠实性常数有O(1/n)界限，表明几何无偏的软阈值化限制了可恢复信号

Conclusion: 相关矩阵的软阈值化在保持正定性的同时会导致特征空间几何坍缩，限制了信号恢复能力，这为相关矩阵阈值化的理论限制提供了重要见解

Abstract: Standard thresholding techniques for correlation matrices often destroy positive semidefiniteness. We investigate the construction of positive definite functions that vanish on specific sets $K \subseteq [-1,1)$, ensuring that the thresholded matrix remains a valid correlation matrix. We establish existence results, define a criterion for faithfulness based on the linear coefficient of the normalized Gegenbauer expansion in analogy with Delsarte's method in coding theory, and provide bounds for thresholding at single points and pairs of points.
  We prove that for correlation matrices of rank $n$, any soft-thresholding operator that preserves positive semidefiniteness necessarily induces a geometric collapse of the feature space, as quantified by an $\mathcal{O}(1/n)$ bound on the faithfulness constant. Such demonstrates that geometrically unbiased soft-thresholding limits the recoverable signal.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Ranking Reasoning LLMs under Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10960)
*Mohsen Hariri,Michael Hinczewski,Jing Ma,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Scorio是一个用于测试时扩展场景下推理大语言模型排名的开源库，提供多种统计排名方法，在数学竞赛基准测试中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展通过为每个提示采样多个输出来评估推理大语言模型，但在此机制下如何对模型进行排名仍然缺乏系统研究。需要形式化测试时扩展下的密集基准排名问题，并提供可靠的统计方法。

Method: 提出Scorio库，实现多种统计排名方法：配对比较模型、项目反应理论模型、投票规则、基于图和谱的方法。在四个奥林匹克风格数学基准（AIME'24、AIME'25、HMMT'25、BrUMO'25）上评估20个推理模型，最多使用80次试验。引入贝叶斯黄金标准作为基准，并探索使用贪婪解码作为经验先验的方法。

Result: 在完整试验（N=80）下，大多数排名方法与贝叶斯黄金标准高度一致（平均Kendall's τ_b = 0.93-0.95），19-34种方法恢复完全相同的排序。在单次试验机制下，最佳方法达到τ_b ≈ 0.86。使用贪婪解码作为经验先验可将N=1时的方差降低16-52%，但当贪婪和随机采样不一致时可能引入偏差。

Conclusion: 该研究确定了在高预算和低预算测试时扩展场景下可靠的排名方法，并发布了开源Scorio库，为推理大语言模型的评估提供了实用的统计工具。

Abstract: Test-time scaling evaluates reasoning LLMs by sampling multiple outputs per prompt, but ranking models in this regime remains underexplored. We formalize dense benchmark ranking under test-time scaling and introduce Scorio, a library that implements statistical ranking methods such as paired-comparison models, item response theory (IRT) models, voting rules, and graph- and spectral-based methods. Across $20$ reasoning models on four Olympiad-style math benchmarks (AIME'24, AIME'25, HMMT'25, and BrUMO'25; up to $N=80$ trials), most full-trial rankings agree closely with the Bayesian gold standard $\mathrm{Bayes}_{\mathcal{U}}@80$ (mean Kendall's $τ_b = 0.93$--$0.95$), and $19$--$34$ methods recover exactly the same ordering. In the single-trial regime, the best methods reach $τ_b \approx 0.86$. Using greedy decoding as an empirical prior ($\mathrm{Bayes}_{\mathbf{R}_0}@N$) reduces variance at $N=1$ by $16$--$52\%$, but can bias rankings when greedy and stochastic sampling disagree. These results identify reliable ranking methods for both high- and low-budget test-time scaling. We release Scorio as an open-source library at https://github.com/mohsenhariri/scorio.

</details>


### [111] [Decoupling Reasoning and Confidence: Resurrecting Calibration in Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09117)
*Zhengzhao Ma,Xueru Wen,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Jinglin Yang,Min He,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DCPO框架通过解耦推理和校准目标，解决了RLVR中存在的校准退化问题，在保持准确性的同时显著改善了过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习从可验证奖励（RLVR）显著提升了大语言模型的推理能力，但会导致严重的校准退化问题，使模型对错误答案过度自信。现有研究试图将校准目标直接纳入现有优化目标，但存在根本性的梯度冲突。

Method: 提出DCPO框架，通过系统性地解耦推理和校准目标来解决梯度冲突问题。该方法避免了直接将校准目标纳入现有优化目标，而是采用分离的优化策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，DCPO在保持与GRPO相当的准确性的同时，实现了最佳的校准性能，显著缓解了过度自信问题。

Conclusion: DCPO为更可靠的大语言模型部署提供了有价值的见解和实用解决方案，通过解耦优化目标有效解决了RLVR中的校准退化问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) significantly enhances large language models (LLMs) reasoning but severely suffers from calibration degeneration, where models become excessively over-confident in incorrect answers. Previous studies devote to directly incorporating calibration objective into existing optimization target. However, our theoretical analysis demonstrates that there exists a fundamental gradient conflict between the optimization for maximizing policy accuracy and minimizing calibration error. Building on this insight, we propose DCPO, a simple yet effective framework that systematically decouples reasoning and calibration objectives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our DCPO not only preserves accuracy on par with GRPO but also achieves the best calibration performance and substantially mitigates the over-confidence issue. Our study provides valuable insights and practical solution for more reliable LLM deployment.

</details>


### [112] [MoE-SpAc: Efficient MoE Inference Based on Speculative Activation Utility in Heterogeneous Edge Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09983)
*Shuhuai Li,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE-SpAc：利用推测解码作为内存管理的前瞻传感器，通过异构负载均衡和异步执行引擎优化MoE模型在边缘设备上的推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘设备上面临严重的内存限制，现有的卸载策略由于自回归专家激活的动态性和低信息特性而受到I/O瓶颈的制约

Method: 提出MoE-SpAc框架：1) 推测效用估计器跟踪专家需求；2) 异构负载均衡器通过在线整数优化动态分区计算；3) 异步执行引擎在统一效用空间协调预取和驱逐

Result: 在七个基准测试中，MoE-SpAc相比最先进的SD基线实现了42%的TPS提升，相比所有标准基线平均加速4.04倍

Conclusion: 将推测解码重新定位为内存管理的信息化前瞻传感器，结合理论分析和实证验证，有效解决了MoE模型在边缘设备推理中的内存约束问题

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable scalable performance but face severe memory constraints on edge devices. Existing offloading strategies struggle with I/O bottlenecks due to the dynamic, low-information nature of autoregressive expert activation. In this paper, we propose to repurpose Speculative Decoding (SD) not merely as a compute accelerator, but as an informative lookahead sensor for memory management, supported by our theoretical and empirical analyses. Hence, we introduce MoE-SpAc, an MoE inference framework that integrates a Speculative Utility Estimator to track expert demand, a Heterogeneous Workload Balancer to dynamically partition computation via online integer optimization, and an Asynchronous Execution Engine to unify the prefetching and eviction in the same utility space. Extensive experiments on seven benchmarks demonstrate that MoE-SpAc achieves a 42% improvement in TPS over the SOTA SD-based baseline, and an average 4.04x speedup over all standard baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/lshAlgorithm/MoE-SpAc .

</details>


### [113] [Personalized Group Relative Policy Optimization for Heterogenous Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10009)
*Jialu Wang,Heinrich Peters,Asad A. Butt,Navid Hashemi,Alireza Hashemi,Pouya M. Ghari,Joseph Hoover,James Rae,Morteza Dehghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: P-GRPO：一种个性化对齐框架，通过解耦优势估计与即时批次统计，针对不同用户偏好进行归一化，解决了标准GRPO在个性化设置中的偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）通常无法与多样化的个人偏好对齐，因为标准的后训练方法（如RLHF）优化的是单一的全局目标。GRPO等基于群体的归一化方法假设所有样本可互换，在个性化设置中会混淆不同用户的奖励分布，系统性地偏向主导偏好而抑制少数信号。

Method: 提出个性化GRPO（P-GRPO）框架，通过将优势估计与即时批次统计解耦来实现。具体方法：不是基于当前生成组进行归一化，而是基于偏好群体特定的奖励历史对优势进行归一化，从而保留学习不同偏好所需的对比信号。

Result: 在多个任务上评估P-GRPO，结果显示它比标准GRPO具有更快的收敛速度和更高的奖励，能够更好地恢复和对齐异构偏好信号。

Conclusion: 在优化层面考虑奖励异质性对于构建能够忠实对齐多样化人类偏好而不牺牲通用能力的模型至关重要。P-GRPO通过解耦优势估计与即时批次统计，有效解决了群体归一化在个性化设置中的偏差问题。

Abstract: Despite their sophisticated general-purpose capabilities, Large Language Models (LLMs) often fail to align with diverse individual preferences because standard post-training methods, like Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF), optimize for a single, global objective. While Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a widely adopted on-policy reinforcement learning framework, its group-based normalization implicitly assumes that all samples are exchangeable, inheriting this limitation in personalized settings. This assumption conflates distinct user reward distributions and systematically biases learning toward dominant preferences while suppressing minority signals. To address this, we introduce Personalized GRPO (P-GRPO), a novel alignment framework that decouples advantage estimation from immediate batch statistics. By normalizing advantages against preference-group-specific reward histories rather than the concurrent generation group, P-GRPO preserves the contrastive signal necessary for learning distinct preferences. We evaluate P-GRPO across diverse tasks and find that it consistently achieves faster convergence and higher rewards than standard GRPO, thereby enhancing its ability to recover and align with heterogeneous preference signals. Our results demonstrate that accounting for reward heterogeneity at the optimization level is essential for building models that faithfully align with diverse human preferences without sacrificing general capabilities.

</details>


### [114] [LWM-Temporal: Sparse Spatio-Temporal Attention for Wireless Channel Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10024)
*Sadjad Alikhani,Akshay Malhotra,Shahab Hamidi-Rad,Ahmed Alkhateeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LWM-Temporal是一个针对无线信道时空特性的基础模型，通过稀疏时空注意力机制和物理信息掩码预训练，学习可重用的通用信道嵌入，在信道预测任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线信道具有复杂的时空特性，传统方法难以有效捕捉移动性引起的信道演化。需要开发一个任务无关的基础模型，学习可重用的通用信道嵌入，以应对各种下游任务。

Method: 在角度-延迟-时间域操作，引入稀疏时空注意力机制，将交互限制在物理可行的邻域内，降低注意力复杂度。采用物理信息掩码课程进行自监督预训练，模拟真实遮挡、导频稀疏和测量损伤。

Result: 在多种移动场景下的信道预测实验中，相比强基线模型取得一致改进，特别是在长时域预测和有限微调数据情况下表现突出。

Conclusion: 几何感知的架构设计和几何一致的预训练对于学习可迁移的时空无线表示至关重要，LWM-Temporal展示了基础模型在无线通信领域的潜力。

Abstract: LWM-Temporal is a new member of the Large Wireless Models (LWM) family that targets the spatiotemporal nature of wireless channels. Designed as a task-agnostic foundation model, LWM-Temporal learns universal channel embeddings that capture mobility-induced evolution and are reusable across various downstream tasks. To achieve this objective, LWM-Temporal operates in the angle-delay-time domain and introduces Sparse Spatio-Temporal Attention (SSTA), a propagation-aligned attention mechanism that restricts interactions to physically plausible neighborhoods, reducing attention complexity by an order of magnitude while preserving geometry-consistent dependencies. LWM-Temporal is pretrained in a self-supervised manner using a physics-informed masking curriculum that emulates realistic occlusions, pilot sparsity, and measurement impairments.
